Entry point for the architecture-scan skill. Proposal-only; it never refactors. Run every few days as entropy control. - **arki**: Bootstrap the initial architecture of a greenfield project from inti's discovery docs: invoke the arki agent to propose a stack (3 options), then generate kuraka.config.yaml, docs/arquitectura/ (ADRs), the .claude/project/ layer skeleton and the source tree. Run after /inti, once its discovery docs are approved. ([CarlosNavarron1990/kuraka](https://github.com/CarlosNavarron1990/kuraka/blob/HEAD/commands/arki.md) · ★4)
- **arkira-init**: First-run setup wizard for the Arkira standards plugin. Collects security and tooling switch preferences, then writes them to disk. ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-init.md))
- **arkira-init-web**: Slim setup wizard for static brochure website repos. Asks a small set of static-web switches, optionally scaffolds the canonical layout. No app-oriented toolchain (no Supabase, Vercel, mobile). ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-init-web.md))
- **arkira-new**: Bootstrap a brand-new project from a brief: wizard, scaffold with the canonical generator, apply Arkira standards, and provision GitHub, Vercel, and Supabase at preview scope. Greenfield counterpart to /intake. ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-new.md))
- **arkira-revendor**: Report that write-enabled re-vendoring is disabled for this release. ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-revendor.md))
- **arkira-role**: Inspect, test, or change Arkira role providers ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-role.md))
- **arkira-sync**: Check or apply Arkira engineering-standards drift in the current repo. ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-sync.md))
- **arkira-update**: Focused re-prompt for Arkira switches added since the last /arkira-init run. ([jeanchastel/arkira](https://github.com/jeanchastel/arkira/blob/HEAD/commands/arkira-update.md))
